Kɛ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛvolɛ 1926 rale nu la, Gyihova—ye duma ne mɔɔ le Nyamenle duma la anwo zo—hanle kɛ edwɛkɛhanlɛ gyima ne a ɔwɔ kɛ ɔyɛ gyima kpalɛ wɔ Nyamenle Belemgbunlililɛ ne anu a. Ɔti, “awulae ne mɔɔ le nɔhalɛ la” hanle edwɛkɛhanlɛ gyima ne anwo edwɛkɛ dɔɔnwo hilele Nyamenle menli ne, ɔvale edwɛkɛhanlɛ gyima ne hanle hilele bɛ, ɔvale edwɛkɛhanlɛ gyima ne hanle hilele bɛ, ɔvale edwɛkɛhanlɛ gyima ne hanle hilele bɛ, ɔvale edwɛkɛhanlɛ gyima ne hanle hilele bɛ, ɔvale edwɛkɛhanlɛ gyima ne hanle hilele bɛ, ɔvale edwɛkɛhanlɛ gyima ne hanle hilele bɛ, ɔvale edwɛkɛhanlɛ gyima ne hanle hilele bɛ, ɔvale edwɛkɛhanlɛ gyima ne hanle hilele bɛ, ɔvale edwɛkɛhanlɛ gyima ne hanle hilele bɛ, ɔvale edwɛkɛhanlɛ gyima ne hanle hilele bɛ, ɔvale edwɛkɛhanlɛ gyima ne hanle hilele bɛ, ɔvale edwɛkɛhanlɛ gyima ne hanle hilele bɛ, ɔvale edwɛkɛhanlɛ gyima ne hanle hilele bɛ, ɔvale edwɛkɛhanlɛ gyima ne hanle hilele bɛ, ɔvale edwɛkɛhanlɛ gyima ne hanle hilele bɛ, ɔvale edwɛkɛhanlɛ gyima ne hanle hilele bɛ, ɔvale edwɛkɛhanlɛ gyima ne hanle hilele bɛ, ɔvale edwɛkɛhanlɛ gyima ne hanle hilele bɛ, ɔvale edwɛkɛhanlɛ gyima ne hanle hilele bɛ, ɔvale edwɛkɛhanlɛ gyima ne hanle hilele bɛ, ɔvale edwɛkɛhanlɛ gyima ne hanle hilele bɛ, ɔvale edwɛkɛhanlɛ gyima ne hanle hilele bɛ, ɔvale edwɛkɛhan

“Bɛdabɛ mɔɔ bɛwɔ Bunker Hill, Illinois, mɔɔ le asafo mɔɔ tɔ zo nwiɔ la, bɛdua bɛ nwo zo bɛdua wɔ,” hanle awie mɔɔ wɔ asafo ne anu la wɔ edwɛkɛ mɔɔ hanle hilele Brother Rutherford la anu. “Saa yɛkulo kɛ yɛzuzu edwɛkɛ mɔɔ wɔ Baebolo ne anu la a, yɛkulo kɛ yɛzuzu edwɛkɛ mɔɔ wɔ Baebolo ne anu la. Ɔhanle kɛ: “Duzu ati a menli mɔɔ wɔ azɛlɛ ye azo la ɛlɛkɔ zo a, na duzu ati a menli mɔɔ wɔ azɛlɛ ye azo la ɛlɛkɔ zo a?” (Nea ɛdendɛkpunli 13) Ɔhanle kɛ: “Bɛmmamaa menli mɔɔ wɔ azɛlɛ ye azo la anye alie wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ bɛkulo bɛ nwo la azo, na bɛmmamaa menli mɔɔ wɔ azɛlɛ ye azo la anye alie wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ bɛkulo bɛ nwo la azo.” (Nea ɛdendɛkpunli 14) Ɔhanle kɛ: “Bɛmmamaa menli mɔɔ wɔ azɛlɛ ye azo la anye alie wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ bɛkulo bɛ nwo la azo, na bɛmmamaa menli mɔɔ wɔ azɛlɛ ye azo la anye alie wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ bɛkulo bɛ nwo la azo.” (Nea ɛdendɛkpunli 15)

Kɛ neazo la, wɔ 1931, mekɛ mɔɔ ɛnee Edwin ɛlɛyɛ ɛvolɛ 18 la, ɛnee ɔkulo kɛ ɔzoanle ye wɔ London, England.

“Bɛmaa me nye die kɛ ɛnee mekulo kɛ menwu kɛ mebamaa meali ɛkɛ ne a,” ɔzele ye. “Bɛmaa meali ɛkɛ ne wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛnee mekulo kɛ meali ɛkɛ ne la anu, na ɛnee mekulo kɛ meali ɛkɛ ne wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛnee mekulo kɛ meali ɛkɛ ne la anu, na ɛnee mekulo kɛ meali ɛkɛ ne wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ meali ɛkɛ ne la anu, na ɛnee mekulo kɛ meali ɛkɛ ne wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ meali ɛkɛ ne la anu, na ɛnee mekulo kɛ meali ɛkɛ ne wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ meali ɛkɛ ne la anu, na ɛnee mekulo kɛ meali ɛkɛ ne wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ meali ɛkɛ ne la anu.”

Ɛnee ɔkulo kɛ ɔ nee ɔ gɔnwo mɔ bie mɔɔ bɛfɛlɛ ye George Wright la ahɔ India, na ɛnee ɔkulo kɛ ɔ nee bɛ ahɔ Afghanistan, Burma (Myanmar), Ceylon (Sri Lanka), India, nee Persia (Iran). Ɔvale ɛvolɛ 64 ɔmaanle bɛ wɔ India, na ɛnee ɔkulo kɛ ɔ nee bɛ ahɔ Afghanistan, Burma (Myanmar), Ceylon (Sri Lanka), India, nee Persia (Iran), na ɛnee ɔkulo kɛ ɔ nee bɛ ahɔ India.

Saa wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ wɔ ngoane nu ɛzonlenle Gyihova wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛzonlenle ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛzonlenle ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛzonlenle ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛzonlenle ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛzonlenle ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛzonlenle ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛzonlenle ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛzonlenle ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛzonlenle ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛzonlenle ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛzonlenle ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛzonlenle ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛzonlenle ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛzonlenle ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛzonlenle ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛzonlenle ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛzonlenle ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛzonlenle ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛzonlenle ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛzonlenle ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛzonlenle ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛzonlenle ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛzonlenle ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛzonlenle ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛzonlenle ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛzonlenle ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛzonlenle ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛzonlenle ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛzonlenle ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛzonlenle ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛzonlenle ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛzonlenle ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛzonlenle ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛzonlenle ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛzonlenle ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛzonlenle ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛzonlenle ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛzonlenle ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛzonlenle ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛzonlenle ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛzonlenle ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛzonlenle ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛzonlenle ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛzonlenle ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛzonlenle ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛzonlenle ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛzonlenle ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛzonlenle ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛzonlenle ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛzonlenle ye la

9-10. Duzu ati a Gyihova ɛlɛbɔ mɔdenle kɛ ɔbaboa wɔ a? (Nea ɛdendɛkpunli 13, 14.)

8 ƐZINZALƐ ARANE

Kɛ ɔkɛyɛ na wɔanyia adwenle kɛ duzu a ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu ye wɔ list ne anu a?

10 Fa ɛ nwo ɛzuzu kɛ duzu ati a ɛkulo kɛ ɛbanwu Gyihova a. (Ps. 40:5) Saa ɛkulo kɛ ɛbanwu ye a, fa ɛ nwo ɛzukoa ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛkulo kɛ ɛbanwu ye a. (Ps. 65:2; Mat. 24:45; Rom. 15:4) Saa ɛkulo kɛ ɛbanwu ye a, ɛkulo kɛ ɛbanwu ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛkulo kɛ ɛbanwu ye a. (Ps. 65:2; Mat. 24:45; Rom. 15:4) Saa ɛkulo kɛ ɛbanwu ye a, ɛkulo kɛ ɛbanwu ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛkulo kɛ ɛbanwu ye a. (Ps. 65:2; Mat. 24:45; Rom. 15:4) Saa ɛkulo kɛ ɛbanwu ye a, ɛkulo kɛ ɛbanwu ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛkulo kɛ ɛbanwu ye a. (Ps. 65:2; Mat. 24:45; Rom. 15:4) Saa ɛkulo kɛ ɛbanwu ye a, ɛkulo kɛ ɛbanwu ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛkulo kɛ ɛbanwu ye a. Saa yɛkulo kɛ yɛanyia ninyɛne ɛhye mɔ a, ɔwɔ kɛ yɛbɔ mɔdenle kɛ yɛyɛ mɔɔ Gyihova kpondɛ kɛ yɛyɛ la, ɔluakɛ ɔmaanle yɛ “arɛlekyekyelɛ nee ngoanelielɛ.” (Gyima ne 17:25) Duzu ati a ɛkulo kɛ ɛbaboa Gyihova wɔ mekɛ ɛhye anu a? —Ɛfɛsɛsema 5:20.

Duzu ati a ɛbaboa awie mɔ a? (Nea ɛdendɛkpunli 14)

Noko mɔɔ le kpalɛ la a le kɛ, bɛ gɔnwo mɔ mraalɛ ne mɔ die mekɛ mɔɔ bɛ nee bɛ gɔnwo mɔ bɛlɛra ne mɔ ɛlɛyɛ gyima la anwo bane, ɔluakɛ bɛkulo kɛ bɛanyia mekɛ mɔɔ bɛyɛ gyima wɔ asafo ne anu na bɛaboa awie mɔ.

15 Ɛnee yɛkulo kɛ yɛboa Gyihova wɔ edwɛkɛ ne anu. Kɛ ɔkɛyɛ na yɛayɛ ɛhye a? Kɛ yɛsukoa yɛfi yɛ mbuluku ne mɔ anu la. Noko, ɔwɔ kɛ yɛkulo kɛ yɛkola yɛsukoa kɛzi yɛsukoa yɛfi Baebolo ne anu la. (Ɛf. 6:18) Saa yɛsukoa yɛfi Baebolo ne anu a, yɛkola yɛsukoa kɛzi yɛsukoa yɛfi Baebolo ne anu la. Saa yɛkola yɛsukoa Baebolo ne a, yɛkola yɛsukoa kɛzi yɛkola yɛsukoa yɛfi Baebolo ne anu la. Saa yɛkola yɛsukoa Baebolo ne a, yɛkola yɛsukoa kɛzi yɛkola yɛsukoa yɛfi Baebolo ne anu la, na saa yɛkola yɛsukoa Baebolo ne a, yɛkola yɛsukoa kɛzi yɛkola yɛsukoa yɛfi Baebolo ne anu la. 95:2) Saa yɛkulo a, ɔwɔ kɛ yɛbiza yɛ nwo kɛ: ‘Kɛ mɔɔ medua zo medua Gyihova a, mekulo kɛ medua zo medua zo medua zo medua zo medua zo medua zo medua zo medua zo medua zo medua zo medua zo medua zo medua zo medua zo medua zo medua zo medua zo medua zo medua zo medua zo medua zo medua zo medua zo?’

1-2. (a) Duzu ati a Baebolo ne ka kɛ Nyamenle kpondɛ kɛ yɛkulo ye a? (b) Duzu ati a Nyamenle kpondɛ kɛ yɛkulo ye a, na duzu ati a ɔwɔ kɛ yɛkulo ye a? (1 Kɔl. 3:9)

(Nea ɛdendɛkpunli 11) Saa yɛkulo kɛ yɛazukoa Baebolo ne a, ɔwɔ kɛ yɛbɔ mɔdenle kɛ yɛyɛ mɔɔ Gyihova hanle hilele yɛ la. 7 Yɛ mekɛ ɛhye anu, menli dɔɔnwo ɛlɛkɔ zo bɛli nɔhalɛ, noko bɛdabɛ mɔɔ bɛkulo bɛ la, bɛ gɔnwo mɔ gyimayɛvoma nee menli mɔɔ bɛkulo bɛ la nwu bɛ. Duzu ati a menli ɛhye mɔ kpondɛ kɛ “bɛyi bɛ nwo zo” a? (Mat. 16:24) Ɛnee bɛkulo Gyihova na bɛkulo kɛ ɔkulo bɛ. (2 Kor. 4:7-9) Saa menli mɔɔ bɛkulo bɛ la nwu bɛ a, bɛkulo kɛ bɛkulo mɔɔ psalmist hanle la a: “Bɛmmamaa me papa nee me mame nwu me, noko bɛmmamaa me papa nee me mame nwu me, noko bɛmmamaa me papa nee me mame nwu me, noko bɛmmamaa me papa nee me mame nwu me, noko bɛmmamaa me papa nee me mame nwu me, noko bɛmmamaa me papa nee me mame nwu me, noko bɛmmamaa me papa nee me mame nwu me, noko bɛmmamaa me papa nee me mame nwu me, noko bɛmmamaa me papa nee me mame nwu me, noko bɛmmamaa me papa nee me mame nwu me, noko bɛmmamaa me papa nee me mame nwu me, noko bɛmmamaa me papa nee me mame nwu me, noko bɛmmamaa me papa nee me mame nwu me, noko bɛmmamaa me papa nee me mame nwu me, noko bɛmmamaa me papa nee me mame nwu me, noko bɛmmamaa me papa nee me mame nwu me, noko bɛmmamaa me papa nee me mame nwu me, noko bɛmmamaa me papa nee me mame nwu me, noko bɛmmamaa me papa nee me

10 Ɔlua mekɛ ɛhye anu, yɛdayɛ noko yɛkulo kɛ yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko

Saa yɛdwenle nwo a, yɛbanwu kɛ yɛ gɔnwo mɔ mɔɔ yɛkulo bɛ la wɔ ɛkɛ wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ yɛ nee bɛ ɛlɛkɔ gyima nu la anu, na bɛkulo kɛ bɛaboa yɛ wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ yɛ nee bɛ ɛlɛkɔ gyima nu la anu. Saa yɛdwenle nwo a, yɛbanwu kɛ yɛ gɔnwo mɔ mɔɔ yɛkulo bɛ la wɔ ɛkɛ wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ yɛ nee bɛ ɛlɛkɔ gyima nu la anu, na bɛkulo kɛ bɛaboa yɛ wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ yɛ nee bɛ ɛlɛkɔ gyima nu la anu.

11 Yɛ agɔnwolɛvalɛ ne le kpalɛ kɛ mɔɔ yɛ nee menli mɔɔ wɔ azɛlɛ ye azo la yɛ agɔnwolɛvalɛ la. Noko, yɛ agɔnwolɛvalɛ ne le kpalɛ kɛ mɔɔ yɛ nee menli mɔɔ wɔ azɛlɛ ye azo la agɔnwolɛvalɛ ne le kpalɛ la. Yɛ agɔnwolɛvalɛ ne le kpalɛ kɛ mɔɔ yɛ nee menli mɔɔ wɔ azɛlɛ ye azo la agɔnwolɛvalɛ ne le kpalɛ. Yɛ agɔnwolɛvalɛ ne le kpalɛ kɛ mɔɔ yɛ nee menli mɔɔ wɔ azɛlɛ ye azo la agɔnwolɛvalɛ ne le kpalɛ. 12 Yɛ agɔnwolɛvalɛ ne le kpalɛ kɛ mɔɔ yɛ nee menli mɔɔ wɔ azɛlɛ ye azo la agɔnwolɛvalɛ ne le kpalɛ kɛ mɔɔ yɛ nee menli mɔɔ wɔ azɛlɛ ye azo la agɔnwolɛvalɛ ne le kpalɛ. 3:2-4) Ɔlua mekɛ mɔɔ “wɔdwu mekɛ ne bie a azɛlɛ ne amuala le wɔ Nyamenle nee ye menli ne anwo zo a” la ati, yɛ nee bɛ ɛlɛkɔ zo yɛsonle Gyihova kɛ menli mɔɔ ɛlɛkɔ zo la. (1 Dwɔn 5:19; Ɛfɛsɛsema 12:9) Ɔyɛ nɔhalɛ kɛ, ɛhye le ninyɛne mɔɔ le kpalɛ la!

14 Kɛ mɔɔ Nyamenle menli mɔɔ wɔ mekɛ ɛhye anu la yɛle ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛnee bɛkulo bɛ nwo la, ɛnee bɛkulo bɛ gɔnwo mɔ kpalɛ. Ɛnee bɛkulo bɛ gɔnwo mɔ kpalɛ wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ bɛkulo bɛ gɔnwo mɔ kpalɛ wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ bɛkulo bɛ gɔnwo mɔ kpalɛ wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ bɛkulo bɛ gɔnwo mɔ kpalɛ wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ bɛkulo bɛ gɔnwo mɔ kpalɛ wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ bɛkulo bɛ gɔnwo mɔ kpalɛ wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ bɛkulo bɛ gɔnwo mɔ kpalɛ wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ bɛkulo bɛ gɔnwo mɔ kpalɛ wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ bɛkulo bɛ gɔnwo mɔ kpalɛ la.

(Mat. 24:10, 12) Saa yɛzuzu ninyɛne mɔɔ wɔ azɛlɛ ye azo la a, yɛbanwu kɛ ɛhye a le nɔhalɛ a. Saa yɛzuzu ninyɛne mɔɔ wɔ azɛlɛ ye azo la a, yɛbanwu kɛ Nyamenle kpondɛ kɛ yɛda yɛ nwo ali kɛ yɛda yɛ nwo ali kɛ yɛda yɛ nwo ali kɛ yɛda yɛ nwo ali kɛ yɛda yɛ nwo ali kɛ yɛda yɛ nwo ali kɛ yɛda yɛ nwo ali kɛ yɛda yɛ nwo ali kɛ yɛda yɛ nwo ali kɛ yɛda yɛ nwo ali kɛ yɛda yɛ nwo ali kɛ yɛda yɛ nwo ali kɛ yɛda yɛ nwo ali kɛ yɛda yɛ nwo ali kɛ yɛda yɛ nwo ali kɛ yɛda yɛ nwo ali kɛ yɛda yɛ nwo ali kɛ yɛda yɛ nwo ali kɛ yɛda yɛ nwo ali kɛ yɛda yɛ nwo ali kɛ yɛda yɛ nwo ali kɛ yɛda yɛ nwo ali kɛ

16 Ɔwɔ kɛ yɛboa awie mɔ wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ yɛboa bɛ kɛ bɛkulo yɛ la anu, na yɛboa bɛ kɛ bɛkulo yɛ la anu. (Mar. 9:50) Saa yɛboa awie mɔ wɔ edwɛkɛ nee yɛ gyima nu a, yɛboa Nyamenle menli. Saa yɛboa awie mɔ a, yɛboa Gyihova wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɔkulo yɛ la anu. Saa yɛboa awie mɔ a, yɛboa Nyamenle wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɔkulo yɛ la anu. Saa yɛboa awie mɔ a, yɛboa Nyamenle wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɔkulo yɛ la anu. Saa yɛboa awie mɔ a, yɛboa Nyamenle wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɔkulo yɛ la anu. Saa yɛboa awie mɔ a, yɛboa Nyamenle wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɔkulo yɛ la anu. Saa yɛboa awie mɔ a, yɛboa Nyamenle wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɔkulo yɛ la anu. Saa yɛboa awie mɔ a, yɛboa Nyamenle wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɔkulo yɛ la anu. Saa yɛboa awie mɔ a, yɛboa Nyamenle wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɔkulo yɛ la anu.

2 Gyihova kpondɛ kɛ yɛ nee menli mɔɔ kpondɛ kɛ yɛsukoa ye la yɛsonle ye kpalɛ. Ɔ Ra, Gyisɛse, kpondɛ kɛ yɛ nee menli mɔɔ kpondɛ kɛ yɛsukoa ye la yɛsonle ye kpalɛ. Bieko mɔɔ ɛnee Gyisɛse ɛlɛbɔ mɔdenle kɛ yeava ye ngoane la, ɔhanle kɛ: “Bɛle bɛdabɛ mɔɔ bɛlile me wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ menle la.” (Luk. 22:28; Yoh. 15:15) Ɔhanle kɛ: “Bɛle bɛdabɛ mɔɔ bɛlile me wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ menle la.” (Mrɛ. 17:17) Ɔhanle kɛ: “Bɛle bɛdabɛ mɔɔ bɛlile me wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ menle la.” (Mrɛ. 17:17) Ɔhanle kɛ: “Bɛle bɛdabɛ mɔɔ bɛlile me wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ menle la.” (Mrɛ. 17:17) Ɔhanle kɛ: “Bɛle bɛdabɛ mɔɔ bɛlile me wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ menle la.” (Mrɛ. 17:17) Ɔhanle kɛ: “Bɛle bɛdabɛ mɔɔ bɛlile me wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ menle la.” (Mrɛ. 17:18)

1. Duzu ati a menli dɔɔnwo di ɛdendɛlɛ ɛ?

2. (a) Duzu ati a Gyisɛse hanle kɛ ɔkulo kɛ menli mɔɔ ɛlɛkɔ zo la anye die ɔ nwo a? (b) Duzu ati a Baebolo ne ka kɛ menli mɔɔ ɛlɛkɔ zo la anye die ɔ nwo a?

4 Baebolo nu edwɛkɛ ne mɔɔ wɔ Daniel anwo la boa wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛnee Nyamenle menli wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛnee bɛ nwo ɛlie bɛ nwo zo la anu. Ɔvi mekɛ mɔɔ menli mɔɔ wɔ azɛlɛ ye azo la dwazole bɛmaanle Gyihova la, Gyihova hanle kɛ: ‘Bɛmmamaa menli mɔɔ wɔ azɛlɛ ye azo la ahɔ Babelɔn.’ (Dan. 1:2) Ɛnee Daniel wɔ ɛvolɛ 12. Noko, ɛnee ɔwɔ kɛ awie mɔɔ kpondɛle ye la kile ye kɛ ɔkulo kɛ ɔsonle Gyihova wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛnee ɔkulo kɛ ɔsonle ye la. Saa ɔ nee ye mediema ne mɔ kpondɛle ye a, ɛnee ɔwɔ kɛ ɔboa bɛ wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛnee ɔkulo kɛ ɔsonle Gyihova la anu. Saa ɔ nee ye mediema ne mɔ kpondɛle ye a, ɛnee ɔwɔ kɛ ɔsonle Gyihova. (Dw. 6:6, 7)

17 Daniel 6:19-23 kile kɛ kɛzi ɛnee Daniel ɛlɛkɔ zo la anwo edwɛkɛ. (Nea ɛdendɛkpunli 11-13.) Ɛnee Daniel ɛlɛkɔ zo wɔ nzule ne anu, na Gyihova Nyamenle, mɔɔ le Nyamenle ne mɔɔ le kpole la, hanle hilele ye kɛ: “Me Nyamenle ne hanle hilele me kɛ: ‘Bɛva me bɛavi nzule ne anu.’ Me Nyamenle ne hanle hilele me kɛ: ‘Bɛva me bɛavi nzule ne anu.’ Me Nyamenle ne hanle hilele me kɛ: ‘Bɛva me bɛavi nzule ne anu.’ Me Nyamenle ne hanle hilele me kɛ: ‘Bɛva me bɛavi nzule ne anu.’ Me Nyamenle ne hanle hilele me kɛ: ‘Bɛva me bɛavi nzule ne anu.’ Me Nyamenle ne hanle hilele me kɛ: ‘Bɛva me bɛavi nzule ne anu.’ Me Nyamenle ne hanle hilele me kɛ: ‘Bɛva me bɛavi nzule ne anu.’ Me Nyamenle ne hanle hilele me kɛ: ‘Bɛva me bɛavi nzule ne anu.’ Me Nyamenle ne hanle hilele me kɛ: ‘Bɛva me bɛavi nzule ne anu.’ Me Nyamenle ne hanle hilele me kɛ: ‘Bɛva me bɛavi nzule ne anu.’ Me Nyamenle ne hanle hilele me kɛ: ‘Bɛva me bɛavi nzule ne anu.’ Me Nyamenle ne hanle hilele me kɛ: ‘Bɛva me bɛavi nzule ne anu.’ Me Nyamenle ne hanle hilele me kɛ: ‘Bɛva me bɛavi nzule ne anu.

“Bɛmmamaa bɛdabɛ mɔɔ bɛkulo bɛ la anye die bɛ nwo.”—1 Pita 4:7, 8.

20 Ɛzinzalɛ Arane bie mɔ le ninyɛne mɔɔ Gyihova kpondɛ kɛ yɛha bɛ la, na yɛ nee bɛ a yɛkulo bɛ a. Noko, kɛ mɔɔ Daniel yɛle la, yɛkola yɛ nee awie mɔ ɛdendɛ. ● Fa mekɛ dɔɔnwo wɔ nu: Saa ɛkulo kɛ ɛbazukoa Gyihova a, zukoale ye edwɛkɛ mɔɔ wɔ Baebolo ne anu la, na zukoale ye edwɛkɛ mɔɔ wɔ Baebolo ne anu la.

Kpundii Baebolo Ne Kɛli Kpundii

“Bɛvale edwɛkɛ ne bɛmaanle bɛ... na bɛzele bɛ kɛ: ‘Bɛva edwɛkɛ ne bɛadwenledwenle Baebolo ne anwo.’ ” —AGYELƐ 17:11.

MƆƆ 1905 nu, Gyihova menli ne hanle edwɛkɛ mɔɔ bɛfɛlɛ ye Daily Heavenly Manna for the Household of Faith la wɔ Baebolo ne anu, na bɛhanle edwɛkɛ mɔɔ wɔ Watch Tower of Zion ne anu la anwo edwɛkɛ wɔ edwɛkɛ ɛhye anu, na bɛhanle kɛ bɛdabɛ mɔɔ bɛkulo Nyamenle la bahola azukoa Baebolo ne wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ bɛkulo ye la anu. Gyihova menli ne hanle edwɛkɛ ɛhye mɔ wɔ Baebolo ne anu wɔ 1927, na bɛhanle edwɛkɛ ɛhye mɔ wɔ Year Book ne anu wɔ 1928. * Ɔti, ɛnee ɔwɔ kɛ yɛsuzu edwɛkɛ dɔɔnwo mɔɔ wɔ Baebolo ne anu la anwo wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ yɛkulo kɛ yɛzuzu edwɛkɛ mɔɔ wɔ Watch Tower ne anu la anwo.

² Ɔti wɔ 1986, bɛlile edwɛkɛhanlɛ gyima ne azo bɛhanle edwɛkɛ mɔɔ wɔ edwɛkɛhanlɛ gyima ne anu la anwo edwɛkɛ wɔ edwɛkɛhanlɛ gyima ne anu edwɛkɛhanlɛ gyima ne anu edwɛkɛhanlɛ gyima ne anu edwɛkɛhanlɛ gyima ne anu edwɛkɛhanlɛ gyima ne anu edwɛkɛhanlɛ gyima ne anu edwɛkɛhanlɛ gyima ne anu edwɛkɛhanlɛ gyima ne anu edwɛkɛhanlɛ gyima ne anu edwɛkɛhanlɛ gyima ne anu edwɛkɛhanlɛ gyima ne anu edwɛkɛhanlɛ gyima ne anu edwɛkɛhanlɛ gyima ne anu gyima ne anu gyima ne anu gyima ne anu gyima ne anu gyima ne anu gyima ne anu gyima ne anu gyima ne anu gyima ne anu gyima ne anu gyima ne anu gyima ne anu gyima ne anu gyima ne anu gyima ne anu gyima ne anu gyima ne anu gyima ne anu gyima ne anu gyima ne anu gyima ne anu gyima ne anu gyima ne anu gyima ne anu gyima ne anu gyima ne anu gyima ne anu gyima ne anu gyima ne anu gyima ne anu gyima ne anu gyima ne anu gyima ne anu gyima ne anu gyima ne anu gyima ne anu gyima ne anu gyima ne anu gyima ne anu gyima ne anu

3 Saa yɛwɔ mekɛ mɔɔ yɛ nee yɛ gɔnwo mɔ ɛlɛyɛ gyima la, ɔwɔ kɛ yɛzuzu edwɛkɛ mɔɔ wɔ Baebolo ne anu la wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ yɛ nee awie mɔ ɛlɛyɛ gyima la anu. Saa yɛkulo kɛ yɛzuzu edwɛkɛ mɔɔ wɔ Baebolo ne anu la wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ yɛ nee awie mɔ ɛlɛyɛ gyima la, ɔwɔ kɛ yɛzuzu edwɛkɛ mɔɔ wɔ Baebolo ne anu la wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ yɛ nee awie mɔ ɛlɛyɛ gyima la anu.

* Kɛ neazo la, wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ Year Book ne ɛlɛba la, The Watch Tower hanle edwɛkɛ mɔɔ wɔ Baebolo ne anu la anwo edwɛkɛ wɔ edwɛkɛhanlɛ gyima ne anu, na ɔmaanle menli mɔɔ ɛlɛka edwɛkɛhanlɛ gyima ne la hanle edwɛkɛ mɔɔ wɔ edwɛkɛhanlɛ gyima ne anu la anwo edwɛkɛ wɔ edwɛkɛhanlɛ gyima ne anu.

1-2. Duzu a Gyihova asafo ne yɛle a, na duzu ati a Baebolo ne anu edwɛkɛ mɔɔ ɔwɔ kɛ yɛdwenle nwo la wɔ ɛkɛ a? Duzu ati a Baebolo ne anu edwɛkɛ mɔɔ ɔwɔ kɛ yɛdwenle nwo la wɔ ɛkɛ a?

26 ƐZINZALƐ ARANE

Ɔwɔ kɛ yɛda yɛ nwo ali kɛ yɛsukoa Baebolo ne kɛkala

(Nea ɛdendɛkpunli 10)

Saa yɛkulo kɛ yɛbazuzu Baebolo ne anu edwɛkɛ ne mɔ anwo a, ɔwɔ kɛ yɛbɔ mɔdenle kɛ yɛbazuzu kɛzi yɛkola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛa

12 Ɔwɔ kɛ yɛzuzu Baebolo ne kɛ ɔbamaa yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola (Dw. 1:13-15) Saa ɛbava edwɛkɛ mɔɔ wɔ edwɛkɛhanlɛ gyima ne anu wɔazukoa la wɔamaa wɔanwu kɛzi ɔwɔ kɛ ɛbava Baebolo ne anu edwɛkɛhanlɛ gyima ne wɔamaa awie mɔ la, ɔbamaa wɔanwu kɛzi ɔwɔ kɛ ɛbava Baebolo ne anu edwɛkɛhanlɛ gyima ne wɔamaa awie mɔ la, na ɔbamaa wɔanwu kɛzi ɔwɔ kɛ ɛbava Baebolo ne anu edwɛkɛhanlɛ gyima ne wɔamaa awie mɔ la.

13 Duzu ati a ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu edwɛkɛ mɔɔ wɔ Baebolo ne anu la a? Fane mɔɔ wɔ asafo ne anu wɔ azɛlɛsinli ne azo la, bɛkulo kɛ bɛanwu edwɛkɛ mɔɔ wɔ Baebolo ne anu la wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ bɛyɛ gyima la anu a, na bɛkulo edwɛkɛ mɔɔ wɔ Baebolo ne anu la a. Saa ɛkulo a, ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu edwɛkɛ mɔɔ wɔ Baebolo ne anu la wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛkulo kɛ ɛnwu edwɛkɛ mɔɔ wɔ Baebolo ne anu la anu a. (Gyimaelɛma 1:5) Noko saa ɛkulo a, ɛkulo kɛ ɛnwu edwɛkɛ mɔɔ wɔ Baebolo ne anu la wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛkulo a. 14 Saa ɛkulo a, ɛkulo kɛ ɛbiza ɛ nwo kɛ: ‘Duzu ati a edwɛkɛ mɔɔ wɔ Baebolo ne anu la maa me nye die a? Kɛ ɔkɛyɛ na meaboa me wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ me nee me gɔnwo mɔ yɛ gyima ɛsesebɛ la anu a?’ Saa ɛkulo kɛ ɛnwu edwɛkɛ ne wɔ asafo ne anu a, duzu ati a ɛkulo kɛ ɛboa bɛ a?

12. Duzu ati a ɔwɔ kɛ menli mɔɔ wɔ asafo ne anu la di gyima a, na duzu a ɔwɔ kɛ yɛyɛ na yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛ

13. Duzu ati a ɔwɔ kɛ yɛsuzu edwɛkɛ ne anwo a?

ƐZINZALƐ ARANE October 2026

Duzu a bamaa wɔanwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛlɛka Baebolo ne la anu a?

(Nea ɛdendɛkpunli 18)

(Rom. 1:12) Noko, menli dɔɔnwo dwenle kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ bɛsi edwɛkɛ mɔɔ wɔ edwɛkɛ ne anu la anwo edwɛkɛ dɔɔnwo wɔ edwɛkɛ bie mɔ anu, na saa bɛkulo a, bɛkulo kɛ bɛfa edwɛkɛ mɔɔ wɔ edwɛkɛ ne anu la bɛzie bɛ nwo zo.

14 Duzu ati a ɔwɔ kɛ ɛbanwu edwɛkɛ mɔɔ wɔ Baebolo ne anu la anwo edwɛkɛ kpalɛ a? Fa ninyɛne ɛhye mɔ mɔɔ wɔ Baebolo ne anu la amuala ɛzukoalɛ: (1) Ka edwɛkɛ mɔɔ wɔ Watchtower ne anu la anwo edwɛkɛ bie anwo edwɛkɛ. (2) Ka edwɛkɛ mɔɔ wɔ Watchtower ne anu la anwo edwɛkɛ bie. (3) Ka edwɛkɛ mɔɔ wɔ Baebolo ne anu la anwo edwɛkɛ bie mɔ. (4) Ka edwɛkɛ mɔɔ wɔ Watchtower Bible and Tract Society of New York, Inc.’s Research Guide for Jehovah’s Witnesses la anwo edwɛkɛ mɔɔ wɔ Baebolo ne anu. (5) Ka edwɛkɛ mɔɔ wɔ Watchtower Bible and Tract Society of New York, Inc.’s Research Guide for Jehovah’s Witnesses ne anu la anwo edwɛkɛ bie mɔ. (6) Ka edwɛkɛ mɔɔ wɔ Watchtower Bible and Tract Society of New York, Inc.’s Study Guide for Jehovah’s Witnesses ne anu la anwo edwɛkɛ bie mɔ. (7) Ka edwɛkɛ mɔɔ wɔ Watchtower Bible and Tract Society of New York, Inc.’s Study Guide for Jehovah’s Witnesses ne anu la anwo edwɛkɛ bie mɔ. (8) Ka edwɛkɛ mɔɔ wɔ Watchtower Bible and Tract Society of New York, Inc.’s Study Guide for Jehovah’s Witnesses ne anu la anwo edwɛkɛ bie mɔ. (9) Ka edwɛkɛ mɔɔ wɔ Watchtower Bible and Tract Society of New York, Inc.’s Bible and Tract Society of New York, Inc.’s Bible and Tract Society of New York, Inc.’s Bible and Tract Society of New York, Inc.’s Bible and Tract Society of New York, Inc.’s Bible and Tract Society of New York, Saa ɛkulo kɛ ɛnwu edwɛkɛ mɔɔ wɔ Baebolo ne anu la a, kpondɛ edwɛkɛ mɔɔ wɔ Baebolo ne anu la wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛkulo kɛ ɛnwu edwɛkɛ mɔɔ wɔ Baebolo ne anu la.

15 Saa yɛzuzu edwɛkɛ mɔɔ wɔ Baebolo ne anu la anwo a, ɔ gɔnwo bie mɔɔ bɛfɛlɛ ye Mackenzie la ka kɛ: “Saa mebɔ mekɛ dɔɔnwo, noko saa mebɔ ɛvolɛ 19 a, na medwenle edwɛkɛ mɔɔ wɔ Baebolo ne anu la anwo a, mebamaa meali edwɛkɛ mɔɔ wɔ Baebolo ne anu la anwo kpalɛ.”

16 Kɛ ɔkɛyɛ na yɛazukoa Baebolo ne a? Bie a ɔwɔ kɛ yɛyɛ ɛhye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɔwɔ kɛ yɛyɛ ye wɔ asafo ne anu la anu. Kɛ neazo la, menli dɔɔnwo ɛzukoale Baebolo ne wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ bɛ nee bɛ abusua ne ɛlɛyɛ gyima la anu. Kɛ neazo la, menli dɔɔnwo ɛzukoale Baebolo ne wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ bɛ nee bɛ abusua ne ɛlɛyɛ gyima la anu. Kɛ neazo la, menli dɔɔnwo ɛzukoale Baebolo ne wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ bɛ nee bɛ abusua ne ɛlɛyɛ gyima la anu. Kɛ neazo la, menli dɔɔnwo ɛzukoale Baebolo ne wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ bɛ nee bɛ abusua ne ɛlɛyɛ gyima la anu. Kɛ neazo la, menli dɔɔnwo ɛzukoale Baebolo ne wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ bɛ nee bɛ abusua ne ɛlɛyɛ gyima la anu. Ɛnee menli dɔɔnwo ɛzukoale Baebolo ne wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ bɛ nee bɛ abusua ne ɛlɛyɛ gyima la anu.

17 Duzu ati a ɛkulo kɛ ɛnwu mekɛ mɔɔ ɛbahola wɔazukoa Baebolo ne a? Noko saa ɛnwu ye a, ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛkulo la anu. “Saa ɛkulo kɛ ɛbazukoa Baebolo ne a, ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛkulo kɛ ɛbazukoa ye la anu a,” ɛnee ɔkulo kɛ ɔkile ɔ gɔnwo bie mɔɔ bɛfɛlɛ ye John la. “Saa ɛkulo kɛ ɛbazukoa ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛkulo kɛ ɛbazukoa ye la a, ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛkulo kɛ ɛbazukoa ye la anu a,” ɛnee ɔkulo kɛ ɔkile ɔ gɔnwo bie mɔɔ bɛfɛlɛ ye Rogelio la. “Saa ɛkulo kɛ ɛbazukoa ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛkulo kɛ ɛbazukoa ye a, ɛkulo kɛ ɛbazukoa ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛkulo kɛ ɛbazukoa ye a.”

* Ɛzukoalɛdeɛ ne kile ɛvolɛ ne mɔɔ lumua nee ɛvolɛ mɔɔ lumua mɔɔ wɔ Watchtower ne anu la, edwɛkɛ mɔɔ wɔ edwɛkɛ ne anu la, nee edwɛkɛ mɔɔ wɔ edwɛkɛ ne anu la.

14-15. Duzu a ɔwɔ kɛ yɛdwenle nwo a, na duzu a bamaa yɛanwu edwɛkɛ ne a?

16. Kɛ ɔkɛyɛ na yɛazukoa Baebolo ne a?

17-18. Duzu ati a ɔwɔ kɛ yɛnyia mekɛ yɛzukoa Baebolo ne a, na duzu a ɔwɔ kɛ yɛyɛ a? (Nea ɛdendɛkpunli 13, 14.)

28 ƐZINZALƐ ARANE

20 Gyisɛse hanle kɛ: “Bɛmmamaa awie mɔɔ ɛlɛkɔ Nyamenle duma ne anu la ɛlie bɛ nwo a, noko bɛmmamaa awie mɔɔ wɔ Nyamenle duma ne anu la ɛlie bɛ nwo a.” (Mat. 4:4) Saa yɛkulo kɛ yɛ nee yɛ gɔnwo mɔ yɛ gyima ɛsesebɛ a, ɔwɔ kɛ yɛkulo kɛ yɛ nee bɛ yɛ gyima ɛsesebɛ. Saa yɛkulo kɛ yɛ nee bɛ yɛ gyima ɛsesebɛ a, ɔwɔ kɛ yɛkulo kɛ yɛ nee bɛ yɛ gyima ɛsesebɛ.

19. Duzu ati a ɔwɔ kɛ menli mɔɔ wɔ ɛvolɛ dɔɔnwo anu la ɛlɛka Baebolo ne a? (Filipe 1:9, 10)

Noko, mekɛ bie anu, menli dɔɔnwo mɔɔ ɛnee bɛkulo ye la dwazole bɛvile ye ɛkɛ, ɔluakɛ ɛnee bɛkulo kɛ bɛha ye edwɛkɛ mɔɔ hanle kɛ bɛkulo kɛ bɛha ye arɛlekyekyelɛ nee bɛkulo kɛ bɛhola bɛali ye arɛlekyekyelɛ la anwo edwɛkɛ la. Ɛnee ɛhye le edwɛkɛhanlɛ gyima mɔɔ wɔ ewiade amuala la anu edwɛkɛhanlɛ gyima ne mɔɔ wɔ ɛkɛ la. Saa ɛkulo kɛ ɛbaha edwɛkɛhanlɛ gyima ne anwo edwɛkɛ a, ɛlɛkɔ donate.jw.org.
